{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-sonnet-4-5-20250929","id":"msg_vrtx_01GADceVzp4yhgkjn7TzwipF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01M9PzWhuh5iMAesd9zNqC45","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/root/environment/skills/julia-jump-optimization.md","content":"# JuMP Optimization Modeling in Julia\n\n## Overview\nJuMP (Julia for Mathematical Programming) is a domain-specific modeling language for mathematical optimization embedded in Julia. It supports linear, mixed-integer, nonlinear, conic, and semidefinite programming.\n\n## Installation\n\n```bash\n# Install Julia first (if not installed)\n# Then install JuMP and solvers\njulia -e 'using Pkg; Pkg.add(\"JuMP\"); Pkg.add(\"Ipopt\")'\n```\n\n## Basic Usage\n\n### Creating a Model\n\n```julia\nusing JuMP\nusing Ipopt\n\n# Create a model with Ipopt solver\nmodel = Model(Ipopt.Optimizer)\n\n# Set solver options\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"print_level\", 5)\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"tol\", 1e-6)\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"max_iter\", 3000)\n```\n\n### Defining Variables\n\n```julia\n# Continuous variables with bounds\n@variable(model, 0 <= x <= 10)\n@variable(model, lb <= y <= ub)\n\n# Variables without bounds\n@variable(model, z)\n\n# Variable arrays\n@variable(model, w[1:n])\n\n# Variables with complex indexing\n@variable(model, v[buses], start=1.0)\n```\n\n### Setting Variable Start Values\n\n```julia\n# Start values help nonlinear solvers\nset_start_value(x, 5.0)\nset_start_value(v[i], 1.0)\n```\n\n### Defining Constraints\n\n```julia\n# Linear constraints\n@constraint(model, x + y <= 10)\n\n# Nonlinear constraints\n@NLconstraint(model, x^2 + y^2 <= 25)\n\n# Constraint arrays\n@constraint(model, con[i=1:n], x[i] + y[i] == b[i])\n\n# Complex nonlinear constraints\n@NLconstraint(model, vm[i]^2 == vr[i]^2 + vi[i]^2)\n```\n\n### Objective Function\n\n```julia\n# Linear objective\n@objective(model, Min, 2x + 3y)\n\n# Quadratic objective\n@objective(model, Min, x^2 + y^2)\n\n# Nonlinear objective\n@NLobjective(model, Min, sum(c2[i] * pg[i]^2 + c1[i] * pg[i] + c0[i] for i in gens))\n```\n\n### Solving and Retrieving Results\n\n```julia\n# Solve the model\noptimize!(model)\n\n# Check termination status\nstatus = termination_status(model)\nprintln(\"Status: \", status)\n\n# Get objective value\nobj = objective_value(model)\n\n# Get variable values\nx_val = value(x)\nv_vals = [value(v[i]) for i in buses]\n\n# Check if solution is optimal\nif status == MOI.OPTIMAL\n    println(\"Optimal solution found\")\nelseif status == MOI.LOCALLY_SOLVED\n    println(\"Locally optimal solution found\")\nend\n```\n\n## Advanced Features\n\n### Nonlinear Expressions\n\n```julia\n# Register custom functions if needed\nsquare(x) = x^2\n@register(model, square(x))\n\n# Use in constraints\n@NLconstraint(model, square(x) + square(y) <= 100)\n```\n\n### Working with Complex Power Flow\n\n```julia\n# For AC power flow, decompose complex variables into real/imaginary\n# or magnitude/angle components\n\n# Polar form (magnitude and angle)\n@variable(model, vm[buses])  # voltage magnitude\n@variable(model, va[buses])  # voltage angle\n\n# Rectangular form\n@variable(model, vr[buses])  # real part\n@variable(model, vi[buses])  # imaginary part\n\n# Relationship: vm^2 = vr^2 + vi^2\n@NLconstraint(model, [i in buses], vm[i]^2 == vr[i]^2 + vi[i]^2)\n```\n\n### Handling Solver Attributes\n\n```julia\n# Common Ipopt options\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"print_level\", 0)  # 0-12, verbosity\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"tol\", 1e-6)       # convergence tolerance\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"max_iter\", 3000)  # max iterations\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"warm_start_init_point\", \"yes\")  # warm start\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"mu_strategy\", \"adaptive\")  # barrier parameter\n```\n\n## Example: Nonlinear Optimization\n\n```julia\nusing JuMP, Ipopt\n\nmodel = Model(Ipopt.Optimizer)\n\n# Variables\n@variable(model, 0 <= x <= 10)\n@variable(model, 0 <= y <= 10)\n\n# Objective\n@NLobjective(model, Min, x^2 + y^2)\n\n# Constraints\n@NLconstraint(model, x * y >= 1)\n@constraint(model, x + y >= 2)\n\n# Solve\noptimize!(model)\n\n# Results\nprintln(\"x = \", value(x))\nprintln(\"y = \", value(y))\nprintln(\"Objective = \", objective_value(model))\n```\n\n## Tips for Power System Optimization\n\n1. **Use good initial points**: Set start values close to expected solution\n2. **Scale variables**: Keep variables in similar magnitude ranges (0.9-1.1 for voltage)\n3. **Reference angle**: Fix at least one voltage angle to zero for reference\n4. **Solver tolerance**: Use 1e-6 to 1e-8 for good accuracy\n5. **Check status**: Always verify termination_status before using results\n\n## Common Pitfall\n\nDon't mix linear and nonlinear syntax:\n```julia\n# WRONG: mixing @ and @NL\n@objective(model, Min, x^2 + y)\n\n# CORRECT for nonlinear:\n@NLobjective(model, Min, x^2 + y)\n\n# CORRECT for linear:\n@objective(model, Min, 2*x + y)\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":320,"cache_read_input_tokens":30515,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":320,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4d936d42-c2c4-49e6-8568-0ba35e6df2d6","uuid":"f8267776-8c6c-4f49-829c-c74651b96add"}

This is more accurate than DC power flow but involves nonlinear equations.\n\n## Fundamental Concepts\n\n### Complex Power\n```julia\n# S = P + jQ (complex power)\n# P = real power (MW)\n# Q = reactive power (MVAr)\n# S = apparent power (MVA), |S| = sqrt(P^2 + Q^2)\n```\n\n### Complex Voltage\n```julia\n# V = V_r + jV_i (rectangular form)\n# V = |V| * e^(jθ) (polar form)\n# |V| = voltage magnitude (per-unit)\n# θ = voltage angle (radians)\n```\n\n### Admittance Matrix\nThe network is represented by the admittance matrix Y, where:\n- Y_ij = complex admittance between buses i and j\n- Y_ii = sum of all admittances connected to bus i\n\n## Power Flow Equations in Polar Form\n\n### Power Injection at Bus i\n\nFor a bus i with voltage V_i = |V_i| e^(jθ_i):\n\n```julia\n# Complex power injection\nS_i = V_i * conj(I_i)\n    = V_i * conj(sum(Y_ij * V_j for j in buses))\n\n# In polar form:\nP_i = |V_i| * sum(|V_j| * (G_ij * cos(θ_i - θ_j) + B_ij * sin(θ_i - θ_j)) for j in buses)\nQ_i = |V_i| * sum(|V_j| * (G_ij * sin(θ_i - θ_j) - B_ij * cos(θ_i - θ_j)) for j in buses)\n\nwhere:\n# Y_ij = G_ij + jB_ij (conductance + susceptance)\n```\n\n### Simplified Form (Self + Neighbors)\n\n```julia\n# Real power at bus i\nP_i = G_ii * V_i^2 + sum(V_i * V_j * (G_ij * cos(θ_ij) + B_ij * sin(θ_ij)) for j in neighbors(i))\n\n# Reactive power at bus i\nQ_i = -B_ii * V_i^2 + sum(V_i * V_j * (G_ij * sin(θ_ij) - B_ij * cos(θ_ij)) for j in neighbors(i))\n\nwhere:\n# θ_ij = θ_i - θ_j (angle difference)\n```\n\n## Branch Power Flow\n\nFor a branch from bus i to bus j with series admittance y_ij and shunt admittances y_ij^c:\n\n```julia\n# Power flow from i to j (with transformer ratio τ_ij = τ * e^(jα))\nS_ij = (y_ij + y_ij^c)^* * |V_i|^2 / |τ_ij|^2 - y_ij^* * V_i * conj(V_j) / conj(τ_ij)\n\n# Power flow from j to i\nS_ji = (y_ij + y_ji^c)^* * |V_j|^2 - y_ij^* * conj(V_i) * V_j / τ_ij\n```\n\n### For lines (no transformer, τ = 1):\n\n```julia\n# From i to j\nP_ij = g_ij * V_i^2 - V_i * V_j * (g_ij * cos(θ_ij) + b_ij * sin(θ_ij))\nQ_ij = -(b_ij + b_ij^c) * V_i^2 - V_i * V_j * (g_ij * sin(θ_ij) - b_ij * cos(θ_ij))\n\n# From j to i\nP_ji = g_ij * V_j^2 - V_i * V_j * (g_ij * cos(θ_ij) - b_ij * sin(θ_ij))\nQ_ji = -(b_ij + b_ji^c) * V_j^2 + V_i * V_j * (g_ij * sin(θ_ij) + b_ij * cos(θ_ij))\n\nwhere:\n# y_ij = g_ij + jb_ij (series admittance)\n# y_ij^c = jb_ij^c (shunt susceptance at bus i)\n```\n\n## Power Balance Constraint\n\nAt each bus, generation minus load equals network injection:\n\n```julia\n# Real power balance\nsum(P_g[g] for g in gens_at_bus_i) - P_load[i] - sum(shunt_G * V_i^2) = sum(P_ij for j in neighbors(i))\n\n# Reactive power balance\nsum(Q_g[g] for g in gens_at_bus_i) - Q_load[i] - sum(shunt_B * V_i^2) = sum(Q_ij for j in neighbors(i))\n```\n\n## Branch Flow Limits\n\n```julia\n# Apparent power limit (MVA)\nsqrt(P_ij^2 + Q_ij^2) <= S_ij^max\n\n# Current magnitude limit\n|I_ij| = |S_ij| / |V_i| <= I_ij^max\n\n# In optimization, use:\nP_ij^2 + Q_ij^2 <= (S_ij^max)^2\n```\n\n## Implementation in JuMP\n\n### Variables\n\n```julia\n@variable(model, va[buses])           # voltage angle (radians)\n@variable(model, vmin[i] <= vm[i=buses] <= vmax[i])  # voltage magnitude (p.u.)\n@variable(model, pgmin[g] <= pg[g=gens] <= pgmax[g]) # real power generation\n@variable(model, qgmin[g] <= qg[g=gens] <= qgmax[g]) # reactive power generation\n```\n\n### Power Balance Constraints\n\n```julia\n# Real power balance at each bus\n@NLconstraint(model, pbal[i in buses],\n    sum(pg[g] for g in gens_at[i]) - pload[i]\n    == sum(vm[i] * vm[j] * (G[i,j] * cos(va[i] - va[j]) + B[i,j] * sin(va[i] - va[j]))\n           for j in buses)\n)\n\n# Reactive power balance at each bus\n@NLconstraint(model, qbal[i in buses],\n    sum(qg[g] for g in gens_at[i]) - qload[i]\n    == sum(vm[i] * vm[j] * (G[i,j] * sin(va[i] - va[j]) - B[i,j] * cos(va[i] - va[j]))\n           for j in buses)\n)\n```\n\n### Reference Angle\n\n```julia\n# Fix reference bus angle to zero\n@constraint(model, va[ref_bus] == 0)\n```\n\n### Branch Flow Calculation (Post-Solve)\n\n```julia\n# After solving, calculate branch flows\nfor (i,j) in branches\n    θ_ij = value(va[i]) - value(va[j])\n    v_i = value(vm[i])\n    v_j = value(vm[j])\n\n    p_ij = g[i,j] * v_i^2 - v_i * v_j * (g[i,j] * cos(θ_ij) + b[i,j] * sin(θ_ij))\n    q_ij = -(b[i,j] + bc[i,j]) * v_i^2 - v_i * v_j * (g[i,j] * sin(θ_ij) - b[i,j] * cos(θ_ij))\n\n    s_ij = sqrt(p_ij^2 + q_ij^2)\nend\n```\n\n## Tips for Numerical Stability\n\n1. **Use per-unit system**: All values in p.u. (base MVA = 100 typically)\n2. **Angles in radians**: Convert degrees to radians for trig functions\n3. **Good initial point**: Start with flat start (vm=1.0, va=0.0) or previous solution\n4. **Scale costs**: If using $/MWh, scale appropriately with base MVA\n5. **Check conditioning**: Very small or large admittance values may cause issues\n\n## Common Issues\n\n1. **Unbounded angles**: Always fix at least one reference angle\n2. **Infeasible voltage limits**: Check if loads are too high for generator capacity\n3. **Poor convergence**: Try better initial point or adjust solver tolerances\n4. **Multiple solutions**: AC OPF is non-convex, different initializations may yield different local optima\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1620,"cache_read_input_tokens":30835,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1620,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4d936d42-c2c4-49e6-8568-0ba35e6df2d6","uuid":"737ea4cd-0ca4-4178-adc4-268f3b1957ea"}

return generators\nend\n\n# Branch data: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax]\nfunction parse_branches(branch_data)\n    branches = []\n    for row in branch)\n    end\n    return branches\nend\n```\n\n## Example: Complete Workflow\n\n```julia\nusing JSON\n\n# Read network data\nnetwork = JSON.parsefile(\"network.json\")\nbuses = Process each bus\nend\n\n# Enumerate with index\nfor (idx, bus) in enumerate(data[\"bus\"])\n    println(\"Bus $idx: \", bus[1])\nend\n\n# List comprehension\nbus_ids = [bus[1] for bus in data[\"bus\"]]\n```\n\n## Writing JSON Files\n\n### Creating JSON from Julia Data\n\n```julia\nusing JSON\n\n# Create a dictionary\nreport = Dict(\n    \"summary\" => Dict(\n        \"total_cost\" => 123456.78,\n        \"total_load_MW\" => 1500.0,\n        \"solver_status\" => \"optimal\"\n    ),\n    \"generators\" => [\n        Dict(\n            \"id\" => 1,\n            \"bus\" => 100,\n            \"pg_MW\" => 245.5,\n            \"qg_MVAr\" => 50.0\n        ),\n        Dict(\n            \"id\" => 2,\n            \"bus\" => 200,\n            \"pg_MW\" => 300.0,\n            \"qg_MVAr\" => 75.0\n        )\n    ]\n)\n\n# Write to file with pretty formatting\nopen(\"report.json\", \"w\") do f\n    JSON.print(f, report, 2)  # 2 spaces indentation\nend\n\n# Or write in compact form\nopen(\"compact.json\", \"w\") do f\n    JSON.print(f, report)\nend\n```\n\n### Converting Julia Objects\n\n```julia\n# Arrays convert directly\njulia_array = [1, 2, 3, 4, 5]\njson_str = JSON.json(julia_array)\n# Result: \"[1,2,3,4,5]\"\n\n# Dictionaries convert to objects\njulia_dict = Dict(\"a\" => 1, \"b\" => 2)\njson_str = JSON.json(julia_dict)\n# Result: '{\"a\":1,\"b\":2}'\n\n# Nested structures\ncomplex_data = Dict(\n    \"buses\" => [\n        Dict(\"id\" => 1, \"vm\" => 1.05),\n        Dict(\"id\" => 2, \"vm\" => 1.03)\n    ]\n)\njson_str = JSON.json(complex_data)\n```\n\n## Formatting Numbers\n\n```julia\n# Control decimal places using round\nreport = Dict(\n    \"voltage\" => round(1.049999, digits=2),  # Here's how to parse it:\n\n```julia\n# Bus data structure: [bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\nfunction parse_buses(bus_data)\n    buses = Dict()\n    for row in bus_data\n        bus_id = Int(row[1])\n        buses[bus_id] = Dict(\n            \"id\" => bus_id,\n            \"type\" => Int(row[2]),\n            \"Pd\" => row[3],\n            \"Qd\" => row[4],\n            \"Gs\" => row[5],\n            \"Bs\" => row[6],\n            \"Vm\" => row[8],\n            \"Va\" => row[9],\n            \"Vmax\" => row[12],\n            \"Vmin\" => row[13]\n        )\n    end\n    return buses\nend\n\n# Generator data: [bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, ...]\nfunction parse_generators(gen_data)\n    generators = []\n    for (idx, row) in enumerate(gen_data)\n        gen = Dict(\n            \"id\" => idx,\n            \"bus\" => Int(row[1]),\n            \"Pg\" => row[2],\n            \"Qg\" => row[3],\n            \"Qmax\" => row[4],\n            \"Qmin\" => row[5],\n            \"Vg\" => row[6],\n            \"Pmax\" => row[9],\n            \"Pmin\" => row[10]\n        )\n\n        # Get cost coefficients if available\n        if length(row) >= 13\n            gen[\"cost2\"] = row[11]\n            gen[\"cost1\"] = row[12]\n            gen[\"cost0\"] = row[13]\n        end\n\n        push!(generators, gen)\n    end\n    return generators\nend\n\n# Branch data: [fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax]\nfunction parse_branches(branch_data)\n    branches = []\n    for row in branch_data\n        branch = Dict(\n            \"from\" => Int(row[1]),\n            \"to\" => Int(row[2]),\n            \"r\" => row[3],\n            \"x\" => row[4],\n            \"b\" => row[5],\n            \"rateA\" => row[6],\n            \"ratio\" => length(row) >= 9 ? row[9] : 0.0,\n            \"angle\" => length(row) >= 10 ? row[10] : 0.0\n        )\n        push!(branches, branch)\n    end\n    return branches\nend\n```\n\n## Example: Complete Workflow\n\n```julia\nusing JSON\n\n# Read network data\nnetwork = JSON.parsefile(\"network.json\")\nbuses = \"id\" => bus_id,\n            \"vm_pu\" => round(value(vm[bus_id]), digits=4),\n            \"va_deg\" => round(rad2deg(value(va[bus_id])), digits=2)\n        )\n        for bus_id in keys(buses)\n    ]\n)\n\n# Write report\nopen(\"report.json\", \"w\") do f\n    JSON.print(f, report, 2)\nend\n```\n\n## Tips\n\n1. **Use `parsefile()` for files**: More convenient than reading then parsing\n2. **Pretty print with indentation**: Makes output human-readable\n3. **Round floating point**: Avoid excessive decimal places in output\n4. **Convert integers**: Use `Int()` for bus IDs to ensure Understanding the network structure and component models is essential for power flow and optimization analysis.\n\n## Network Components\n\n### Buses (Nodes)\nBuses are connection points in the power system where components are connected.\n\n**Bus Types:**\n1. **Slack/Reference Bus (Type 3)**: Voltage magnitude and angle are fixed (usually V=1.0, θ=0°)\n2. **PV Bus/Generator Bus (Type 2)**: Real power P and voltage magnitude V are specified\n3. **PQ Bus/Load Bus (Type 1)**: Real power P and reactive power Q are specified\n\n**Bus Data Format** (typical MATPOWER-style):\n```\n[bus_i, type, Pd, Qd, Gs, Bs, area, Vm, Va, baseKV, zone, Vmax, Vmin]\n\nbus_i  - bus number (positive integer)\ntype   - bus type (1=PQ, 2=PV, 3=ref)\nPd     - real power demand (MW)\nQd     - reactive power demand (MVAr)\nGs     - shunt conductance (MW at V=1.0 p.u.)\nBs     - shunt susceptance (MVAr at V=1.0 p.u.)\narea   - area number\nVm     - voltage magnitude (p.u.)\nVa     - voltage angle (degrees)\nbaseKV - base voltage (kV)\nzone   - loss zone\nVmax   - maximum voltage magnitude (p.u.)\nVmin   - minimum voltage magnitude (p.u.)\n```\n\n### Generators\nGenerators inject real and reactive power into the network.\n\n**Generator Data Format:**\n```\n[bus, Pg, Qg, Qmax, Qmin, Vg, mBase, status, Pmax, Pmin, cost2, cost1, cost0]\n\nbus    - bus number\nPg     - real power output (MW)\nQg     - reactive power output (MVAr)\nQmax   - maximum reactive power (MVAr)\nQmin   - minimum reactive power (MVAr)\nVg     - voltage setpoint (p.u.)\nmBase  - machine base MVA\nstatus - 1 = in-service, 0 = out-of-service\nPmax   - maximum real power (MW)\nPmin   - minimum real power (MW)\ncost2  - quadratic cost coefficient ($/MW²/h)\ncost1  - linear cost coefficient ($/MW/h)\ncost0  - constant cost coefficient ($/h)\n```\n\n**Generator Cost Function:**\n```\nCost = cost2 * Pg² + cost1 * Pg + cost0  ($/h)\n```\n\n### Branches (Transmission Lines and Transformers)\nBranches connect buses and allow power flow between them.\n\n**Branch Data Format:**\n```\n[fbus, tbus, r, x, b, rateA, rateB, rateC, ratio, angle, status, angmin, angmax]\n\nfbus   - \"from\" bus number\ntbus   - \"to\" bus number\nr      - resistance (p.u.)\nx      - reactance (p.u.)\nb      - total line charging susceptance (p.u.)\nrateA  - MVA rating A (long term)\nrateB  - MVA rating B (short term)\nrateC  - MVA rating C (emergency)\nratio  - transformer off-nominal turns ratio (0 = line)\nangle  - transformer phase shift angle (degrees)\nstatus - 1 = in-service, 0 = out-of-service\nangmin - minimum angle difference (degrees)\nangmax - maximum angle difference (degrees)\n```\n\n**Branch Model (π-model):**\n```\n     i ----[r + jx]---- j\n     |                  |\n    [gc+jbc/2]      [gc+jbc/2]\n     |                  |\n    ---                ---\n\nSeries admittance:  y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\nShunt admittance:   yc = j*b/2 (split between both ends)\n\nFor transformers with tap ratio τ and phase shift φ:\nτ = ratio * e^(j*angle)\n```\n\n## Admittance Matrix Construction\n\nThe network admittance matrix Y is fundamental to power flow analysis.\n\n### Diagonal Elements (Self-Admittance)\n```julia\n# Y[i,i] = sum of all admittances connected to bus i\nY[i,i] = sum(y_ik for all branches connected to i) + shunt_admittance[i]\n```\n\n### Off-Diagonal Elements (Mutual Admittance)\n```julia\n# Y[i,j] = negative of branch admittance between i and j\nY[i,j] = -y_ij  (if branch exists)\nY[i,j] = 0      (if no branch)\n```\n\n### Construction Algorithm\n```julia\nfunction build_ybus(buses, branches)\n    n = length(buses)\n    Y = zeros(ComplexF64, n, n)\n\n    for branch in branches\n        i = branch.from\n        j = branch.to\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y = 1.0 / (branch.r + im*branch.x)\n\n        # Shunt admittance (half at each end)\n        yc = im * branch.b / 2.0\n\n        # Add to matrix\n        Y[i,i] += y + yc\n        Y[j,j] += y + yc\n        Y[i,j] -= y\n        Y[j,i] -= y\n    end\n\n    # Add shunt elements at buses\n    for bus in buses\n        i = bus.id\n        Y[i,i] += bus.Gs + im*bus.Bs\n    end\n\n    return Y\nend\n```\n\n### Extracting G and B matrices\n```julia\nG = real.(Y)  # Conductance matrix\nB = imag.(Y)  # Susceptance matrix\n```\n\n## Per-Unit System\n\nPower systems use per-unit (p.u.) normalization to simplify calculations.\n\n**Base Values:**\n```\nBase MVA (S_base)      - typically 100 MVA\nBase Voltage (V_base)  - system nominal voltage (kV)\nBase Impedance: Z_base = V_base² / S_base\nBase Current:   I_base = S_base / V_base\n```\n\n**Conversions:**\n```julia\n# Voltage: p.u. = actual_kV / base_kV\nvm_pu = voltage_kV / base_kV\n\n# Power: p.u. = actual_MVA / base_MVA\np_pu = power_MW / base_MVA\nq_pu = power_MVAr / base_MVA\n\n# Impedance: p.u. = actual_Ω / base_Ω\nz_pu = z_actual / (base_kV^2 / base_MVA)\n```\n\n**Why Use Per-Unit:**\n1. Simplifies calculations across different voltage levels\n2. Typical values are close to 1.0\n3. Easier to spot errors (voltages around 0.95-1.05)\n4. Transformer ratios are simplified\n\n## Network Analysis Patterns\n\n### Finding Generators at Each Bus\n```julia\ngens_at_bus = Dict{Int, Vector{Int}}()\nfor (i, gen) in enumerate(generators)\n    bus = gen.bus\n    if !haskey(gens_at_bus, bus)\n        gens_at_bus[bus] = Int[]\n    end\n    push!(gens_at_bus[bus], i)\nend\n```\n\n### Finding Branches Connected to a Bus\n```julia\nbranches_at_bus = Dict{Int, Vector{Int}}()\nfor (i, branch) in enumerate(branches)\n    # From side\n    if !haskey(branches_at_bus, branch.from)\n        branches_at_bus[branch.from] = Int[]\n    end\n    push!(branches_at_bus[branch.from], i)\n\n    # To side\n    if !haskey(branches_at_bus, branch.to)\n        branches_at_bus[branch.to] = Int[]\n    end\n    push!(branches_at_bus[branch.to], i)\nend\n```\n\n### Identifying Reference Bus\n```julia\nref_bus = findfirst(bus -> bus.type == 3, buses)\n# Or from data array\nref_bus = findfirst(bus_data -> bus_data[2] == 3, network[\"bus\"])\n```\n\n## Loading Analysis\n\n### Branch Loading Percentage\n```julia\n# Flow as percentage of thermal limit\nloading_pct = (apparent_flow_MVA / rating_MVA) * 100\n\n# Check for violations\nif loading_pct > 100\n    println(\"Branch overloaded: $(loading_pct)%\")\nend\n```\n\n### Most Loaded Branches\n```julia\n# Calculate all loadings\nloadings = [(branch_id, flow_MVA / rating_MVA * 100) for ...]\n\n# Sort by loading\nsort!(loadings, by = x -> x[2], rev = true)\n\n# Get top N\nmost_loaded = loadings[1:N]\n```\n\n## Common Validation Checks\n\n```julia\n# Check power balance\ntotal_gen = sum(pg)\ntotal_load = sum(pd)\ntotal_loss = total_gen - total_load\nprintln(\"Losses: $(total_loss) MW ($(total_loss/total_gen*100)%)\")\n\n# Check voltage violations\nfor bus in buses\n    if vm[bus] < vmin[bus] || vm[bus] > vmax[bus]\n        println(\"Voltage violation at bus $(bus)\")\n    end\nend\n\n# Check branch overloads\nfor branch in branches\n    if flow[branch] > limit[branch]\n        println(\"Branch overload: $(branch)\")\n    end\nend\n```\n\n## Tips for Network Analysis\n\n1. **Use sparse matrices** for large networks (thousands of buses)\n2. **Index by bus number** not array position (bus IDs may not be sequential)\n3. **Check connectivity** ensure network is connected (no islands)\n4. **Validate data** check for zero impedances, negative limits\n5. **Handle transformers** properly account for tap ratios and phase shifts\n6. **Reference bus** always needed for power flow (fixes angle reference)\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":3,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2348,"cache_read_input_tokens":34858,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2348,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"4d936d42-c2c4-49e6-8568-0ba35e6df2d6","uuid":"88e36b26-2a2f-4de9-9875-b9633953546d"}

typically 100 MVA\nBase Voltage (V_base)  - system nominal voltage (kV)\nBase Impedance: Z_base = V_base² / S_base\nBase Current:   I_base = S_base / V_base\n```\n\n**Conversions:**\n```julia\n# Voltage: p.u. = actual_kV / base_kV\nvm_pu = voltage_kV / base_kV\n\n# Power: p.u. = actual_MVA / base_MVA\np_pu = power_MW / base_MVA\nq_pu = power_MVAr / base_MVA\n\n# Impedance: p.u. = actual_Ω / base_Ω\nz_pu = z_actual / (base_kV^2 / base_MVA)\n```\n\n**Why Use Per-Unit:**\n1. Simplifies calculations across different voltage levels\n2. Typical values are close to 1.0\n3. Easier to spot errors (voltages around 0.95-1.05)\n4. Transformer ratios are simplified\n\n## Network Analysis Patterns\n\n### Finding Generators at Each Bus\n```julia\ngens_at_bus = Dict{Int, Vector{Int}}()\nfor (i, gen) in enumerate(generators)\n    bus = gen.bus\n    if !haskey(gens_at_bus, bus)\n        gens_at_bus[bus] = Int[]\n    end\n    push!(gens_at_bus[bus], i)\nend\n```\n\n### Finding Branches Connected to a Bus\n```julia\nbranches_at_bus = Dict{Int, Vector{Int}}()\nfor (i, branch) in enumerate(branches)\n    # From side\n    if !haskey(branches_at_bus, branch.from)\n        branches_at_bus[branch.from] = Int[]\n    end\n    push!(branches_at_bus[branch.from], i)\n\n    # To side\n    if !haskey(branches_at_bus, branch.to)\n        branches_at_bus[branch.to] = Int[]\n    end\n    push!(branches_at_bus[branch.to], i)\nend\n```\n\n### Identifying Reference Bus\n```julia\nref_bus = findfirst(bus -> bus.type == 3, buses)\n# Or from data array\nref_bus = findfirst(bus_data -> bus_data[2] == 3, network[\"bus\"])\n```\n\n## Loading Analysis\n\n### Branch Loading Percentage\n```julia\n# Flow as percentage of thermal limit\nloading_pct = (apparent_flow_MVA / rating_MVA) * 100\n\n# Check for violations\nif loading_pct > 100\n    println(\"Branch overloaded: $(loading_pct)%\")\nend\n```\n\n### Most Loaded Branches\n```julia\n# Calculate all loadings\nloadings = [(branch_id, flow_MVA / rating_MVA * 100) for ...]\n\n# Sort by loading\nsort!(loadings, by = x -> x[2], rev = true)\n\n# Get top N\nmost_loaded = loadings[1:N]\n```\n\n## Common Validation Checks\n\n```julia\n# Check power balance\ntotal_gen = sum(pg)\ntotal_load = sum(pd)\ntotal_loss = total_gen - total_load\nprintln(\"Losses: $(total_loss) MW ($(total_loss/total_gen*100)%)\")\n\n# Check voltage violations\nfor bus in buses\n    if vm[bus] < vmin[bus] || vm[bus] > vmax[bus]\n        println(\"Voltage violation at bus $(bus)\")\n    end\nend\n\n# Check branch overloads\nfor branch in branches\n    if flow[branch] > limit[branch]\n        println(\"Branch overload: $(branch)\")\n    end\nend\n```\n\n## Tips for Network Analysis\n\n1. **Use sparse matrices** for large networks (thousands of buses)\n2. **Index by bus number** not array position (bus IDs may not be sequential)\n3. **Check connectivity** ensure network is connected (no islands)\n4. **Validate data** check for zero impedances, negative limits\n5. **Handle transformers** properly account for tap ratios and phase shifts\n6. **Reference bus** always needed for power flow (fixes angle reference)\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Round up\nmax(x, y)      # Maximum\nmin(x, y)      # Minimum\nsum(arr)       # Sum of array\n```\n\n## File I/O\n\n```julia\n# Read text file\ncontent = read(\"file.txt\", String)\n\n# Write text file\nopen(\"output.txt\", \"w\") do file\n    write(file, \"Hello, World!\")\nend\n\n# Line by line\nopen(\"data.txt\") do file\n    for line in eachline(file)\n        println(line)\n    end\nend\n```\n\n## Common Patterns\n\n### Enumerate\n```julia\nfor (index, value) in enumerate([\"a\", \"b\", \"c\"])\n    println(\"$index: $value\")\nend\n# Output: 1: a, 2: b, 3: c\n```\n\n### Zip\n```julia\nnames = [\"Alice\", \"Bob\"]\nages = [30, 25]\nfor (name, age) in zip(names, ages)\n    println(\"$name is $age\")\nend\n```\n\n### Filter and Map\n```julia\n# Filter\nevens = filter(x -> x % 2 == 0, [1,2,3,4,5,6])  # Descending\nsort(people, by = p -> p.age)          # By field\n```\n\n## Tips for Coming from Python/MATLAB\n\n1. **Indexing starts at 1** not 0\n2. **End keyword** use `end` not -1 for last element\n3. **Arrays are column-major** like MATLAB (affects performance)\n4. **No automatic broadcasting** use `.` for element-wise operations: `arr .+ 1`\n5. **Functions don't need return** last expression is returned\n6. **Type annotations optional** but can improve performance\n7. **Multiple dispatch** functions specialize based on argument types\n\n## Running Julia Code\n\n```bash\n# Interactive REPL\njulia\n\n# Run script\njulia script.jl\n\n# Run command\njulia -e 'println(\"Hello\")'\n\n# With optimizations\njulia -O3 script.jl\n\n# Multi-threading\njulia -t 4 script.jl\n```\n\n## Debugging\n\n```julia\n# Print debugging\nprintln(\"x = $x\")\n@show x           # admittance\n    yc = im * branch[\"b\"] / 2.0\n\n    # Transformer ratio\n    τ = branch[\"ratio\"] * exp(im * branch[\"angle\"])\n\n    # Add to Y-matrix (simplified for τ ≈ 1)\n    if abs(τ - 1.0) < 1e-6\n        Y[i,i] += y + yc\n        Y[j,j] += y + yc\n        Y[i,j] -= y\n        Y[j,i] -= y\n    else\n        # Transformer model\n        Y[i,i] += (y + yc) / abs(τ)^2\n        Y[j,j] += y + yc\n        Y[i,j] -= y / conj(τ)\n        Y[j,i] -= y / τ\n    end\nend\n\n# Add shunt elements\nfor (idx, bus_id) in enumerate(bus_ids)\n    bus = buses[bus_id]\n    Y[idx, idx] += bus[\"Gs\"] + im * bus[\"Bs\"]\nend\n\nG = real.(Y)\nB = imag.(Y)\n\n# Find reference bus\nref_bus_id = findfirst(id -> buses[id][\"type\"] == 3, bus_ids)\nref_bus_idx = bus_idx_map[ref_bus_id]\n\nprintln(\"Building optimization model...\")\nmodel = Model(Ipopt.Optimizer)\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"print_level\", 5)\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"tol\", 1e-6)\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"max_iter\", 3000)\nset_optimizer_attribute(model, \"warm_start_init_point\", \"yes\")\n\n# Variables\n@variable(model, va[1:n])\n@variable(model, vm[i=1:n], start=1.0)\n@variable(model, pg[g=1:length(generators)])\n@variable(model, qg[g=1:length(generators)])\n\n# Set voltage bounds\nfor (idx, bus_id) in enumerate(bus_ids)\n    bus = buses[bus_id]\n    set_lower_bound(vm[idx], bus[\"Vmin\"])\n    set_upper_bound(vm[idx], bus[\"Vmax\"])\nend\n\n# Set generator bounds\nfor (g, gen) in enumerate(generators)\n    set_lower_bound(pg[g], gen[\"Pmin\"])\n    set_upper_bound(pg[g], gen[\"Pmax\"])\n    set_lower_bound(qg[g], gen[\"Qmin\"])\n    set_upper_bound(qg[g], gen[\"Qmax\"])\n    set_start_value(pg[g], (gen[\"Pmin\"] + gen[\"Pmax\"]) / 2)\n    set_start_value(qg[g], 0.0)\nend\n\n# Set voltage start values\nfor (idx, bus_id) in enumerate(bus_ids)\n    bus = buses[bus_id]\n    set_start_value(vm[idx], bus[\"Vm\"])\n    set_start_value(va[idx], bus[\"Va\"])\nend\n\n# Fix reference bus angle\n@constraint(model, va[ref_bus_idx] == 0)\n\n# Objective: minimize generation cost\n@NLobjective(model, Min,\n    sum(generators[g][\"cost2\"] * pg[g]^2 +\n        generators[g][\"cost1\"] * pg[g] +\n        generators[g][\"cost0\"]\n        for g in 1:length(generators))\n)\n\n# Power balance constraints\nprintln(\"Adding power balance constraints...\")\nfor (idx, bus_id) in enumerate(bus_ids)\n    bus = buses[bus_id]\n\n    # Generation at this bus\n    gen_p = haskey(gens_at_bus, bus_id) ?\n        @expression(model, sum(pg[g] for g in gens_at_bus[bus_id])) : 0.0\n    gen_q = haskey(gens_at_bus, bus_id) ?\n        @expression(model, sum(qg[g] for g in gens_at_bus[bus_id])) : 0.0\n\n    # Real power balance\n    @NLconstraint(model,\n        gen_p - bus[\"Pd\"] - bus[\"Gs\"] * vm[idx]^2 ==\n        sum(vm[idx] * vm[j] * (G[idx,j] * cos(va[idx] - va[j]) +\n                               B[idx,j] * sin(va[idx] - va[j]))\n            for j in 1:n)\n    )\n\n    # Reactive power balance\n    @NLconstraint(model,\n        gen_q - bus[\"Qd\"] + bus[\"Bs\"] * vm[idx]^2 ==\n        sum(vm[idx] * vm[j] * (G[idx,j] * sin(va[idx] - va[j]) -\n                               B[idx,j] * cos(va[idx] - va[j]))\n            for j in 1:n)\n    )\nend\n\n# Solve\nprintln(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\nprintln(\"=\"^60)\noptimize!(model)\nprintln(\"=\"^60)\n\n# Check solution status\nstatus = termination_status(model)\nprintln(\"\\nSolver status: \", status)\n\nif status == MOI.OPTIMAL || status == MOI.LOCALLY_SOLVED\n    println(\"Solution found!\")\n\n    # Extract results\n    pg_vals = [value(pg[g]) for g in 1:length(generators)]\n    qg_vals = [value(qg[g]) for g in 1:length(generators)]\n    vm_vals = [value(vm[i]) for i in 1:n]\n    va_vals = [value(va[i]) for i in 1:n]\n\n    # Calculate totals\n    total_gen_MW = sum(pg_vals) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = sum(qg_vals) * baseMVA\n    total_load_MW = sum(buses[id][\"Pd\"] for id in bus_ids) * baseMVA\n    total_load_MVAr = sum(buses[id][\"Qd\"] for id in bus_ids) * baseMVA\n    total_loss_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n    total_cost = objective_value(model)\n\n    # Calculate branch flows\n    println(\"\\nCalculating branch flows...\")\n    branch_flows = []\n    for branch in branches\n        i_id = branch[\"from\"]\n        j_id = branch[\"to\"]\n        i = bus_idx_map[i_id]\n        j = bus_idx_map[j_id]\n\n        vi = vm_vals[i]\n        vj = vm_vals[j]\n        θi = va_vals[i]\n        θj = va_vals[j]\n        θij = θi - θj\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y = 1.0 / (branch[\"r\"] + im * branch[\"x\"])\n        g = real(y)\n        b = imag(y)\n        bc = branch[\"b\"]\n\n        # Power flow from i to j\n        p_ij = g * vi^2 - vi * vj * (g * cos(θij) + b * sin(θij))\n        q_ij = -(b + bc/2) * vi^2 - vi * vj * (g * sin(θij) - b * cos(θij))\n        s_ij = sqrt(p_ij^2 + q_ij^2)\n\n        # Power flow from j to i\n        p_ji = g * vj^2 - vi * vj * (g * cos(θij) - b * sin(θij))\n        q_ji = -(b + bc/2) * vj^2 + vi * vj * (g * sin(θij) + b * cos(θij))\n        s_ji = sqrt(p_ji^2 + q_ji^2)\n\n        # Loading percentage\n        limit = branch[\"rateA\"]\n        loading = limit > 0 ? max(s_ij, s_ji) / limit * 100 : 0.0\n\n        push!(branch_flows, Dict(\n            \"from\" => i_id,\n            \"to\" => j_id,\n            \"s_ij\" => s_ij * baseMVA,\n            \"s_ji\" => s_ji * baseMVA,\n            \"loading\" => loading,\n            \"limit\" => limit * baseMVA\n        ))\n    end\n\n    # Find most loaded branches\n    sort!(branch_flows, by = x -> x[\"loading\"], rev = true)\n    most_loaded = branch_flows[1:min(10, length(branch_flows))]\n\n    # Calculate feasibility metrics\n    max_p_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0.0\n    max_voltage_violation = 0.0\n\n    for (idx, bus_id) in enumerate(bus_ids)\n        bus = buses[bus_id]\n        v = vm_vals[idx]\n\n        # Check voltage violations\n        if v < bus[\"Vmin\"]\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, bus[\"Vmin\"] - v)\n        elseif v > bus[\"Vmax\"]\n            max_voltage_violation = max(max_voltage_violation, v - bus[\"Vmax\"])\n        end\n    end\n\n    max_branch_overload = 0.0\n    for bf in branch_flows\n        if bf[\"loading\"] > 100\n            overload = max(bf[\"s_ij\"], bf[\"s_ji\"]) - bf[\"limit\"]\n            max_branch_overload = max(max_branch_overload, overload)\n        end\n    end\n\n    # Create report\n    println(\"\\nCreating report...\")\n    report = Dict(\n        \"summary\" => Dict(\n digits=2),\n            \"solver_status\" => string(status)\n        ),\n        \"generators\" => [\n            Dict(\n                \"id\" => gen[\"id\"],\n                \"bus\" =>      \"vmin_pu\" => round(buses[bus_id][\"Vmin\"], digits=2),\n                \"vmax_pu\" => round(buses[bus_id][\"Vmax\"], digits=2)\n            )\n            for bus_id in bus_ids\n        ],\n
